PCW — Card

Sabotage 19, 23 September 2001

23 September 2001
Content noticeA period document, served unedited. It carries the humor, language, and content typical of its era and community — some of it crude, dated, or offensive, including material that wouldn't be written today. See Curation.
Sabotage 19 23 Sep 2001 PCW Anniversary Show PCW opens with a slot machine, chaos in a casino, and the arena is ready PCW Anniversary ShowSeptember 23, 2001The scene opens to show a close up of a slot machine with its three reels turning. The middle reel stops and reveals a letter "C" then finally the third reel stops revealing a "W" and after a few tense seconds, the first reel stops on a "P." Bells and whistles go off and a blinking jackpot sign begins flashing as quarter's pour out of the machine. Hundreds of old people rush forward like rabid animals and begin scooping up handfuls of quarters, then things start to get a little rough. It first starts off as pushing and shoving, then a full-fledged geriatric brawl breaks out. The camera shot begins to pan out, as casino security officials run in and try to quell the mayhem. The scene fades out and a shot of the interior of the jam packed PCW Pavilion is shown. Red pyros shoot up from the ring posts and entryways, as some "house" music starts to play... Dave Kern: We are live from the PCW Pavilion in America's Playground, Atlantic City, NJ and it is our pleasure to welcome all of you to the televised debut of Primetime Championship Wrestling and Friday Fusion! I am Dave Kern and my partner in crime is none other than Steven Smith. I gotta tell you, Steven, that these PCW fans will not be disappointed by the action they will witness tonight. Steven Smith: That's right, Dave. Tonight's debut card contains several intriguing matches featuring some of the top wrestlers in the world, plus I am sure PCW management has more than a few surprises up their sleeves for the fans at home. Dave: Surprises or no surprises, this is one heck of a lineup tonight and it will be capped off by a main event match-up between Kremmen and The Third Degree. A TTD chant starts up in the crowd. The camera zooms around the arena to show off the enthusiastic, sign-toting crowd, then finally settles on a handsome looking man in the front row who appears completely unfazed and unimpressed by what's going on around him. Dave: That, ladies and gentleman, is a picture of a selfish and unemployed wrestler. Steven: Dave, when you are as good as Jason Chase, you can be selfish... A Harley Davidson and a powder blue Cadillac Dave: Wait a minute, we are being told that TTD (The crowd pops), is arriving at the arena. Let's go to the back. The scene shifts to the back where TTD is on his way pulling into the driveway of the arena on his Harley Davidson. He revs the engine a few times then speeds towards the entryway of the arena. Then, suddenly, from out of nowhere, a powder blue Cadillac El Dorado screeches out of a parking space and speeds towards TTD. TTD sees it coming and tries to leap from the bike, but the car connects with the bike as he is jumping off, sending TTD tumbling to the ground. The Caddy stops, the door opens and out steps Prisoner 61185. TTD is still on the ground regaining his senses. Prisoner 61185: Mother f^cker! Prisoner 9021-mother f^ckin' O? Kremmen's bitch? What you gotta say now, punk? 61185 begins stomping on the downed TTD. Several security people rush out and pull 61185 off of TTD. Dave: I guess Prisoner 61185 took exception to some of the comments made by TTD earlier in the week. Steven: (sarcastically) Either that, or Prisoner 61185 isn't a very attentive driver... Razor vs Freak of Nature Dave: Looks like we are ready for our first ever match here in Primetime Championship Wrestling, so let's go to the ring! There is a sound of a shotgun and then 28 days' "Rip It Up" hits the speakers as a tall, muscular man with a black mohawk and tattoo of a razor across his back comes to the ring while flipping the bird to the crowd. Clay Clayborne: Making his way to the ring, hailing from Comptom, weighing in at 280 lbs, Razor! The lights dim to green and smoke fills the arena as "Unforgiven" by Metallica hits the PA system. A muscular 6' tall man emerges from the back and walks to the ring with black leather whip in his left hand. Clay Clayborne: And his opponent, hailing from somewhere in the rain forest, weighing in at 215 lbs, Freak of Nature Steven: I bet Peter Puffer would be interested in setting up a little match with FoN and his whip. Dave: Oh stop it! Razor waits climbs the backs away as FoN enters the ring and points to the whip. Referee Buzz Meacham tells FoN to jettison the whip and he obliges. The bell rings and the two men circle. The bigger Razor holds out his right hand and wiggles his fingers, tempting FoN into a game of strength. FoN bites and reaches out, only to receive a quick kick to the stomach for his troubles. Razor wastes no time and lays a double axe handle on FoN, knocking him to the canvas, then stomps on his hand three times before the ref admonishes RazorRazor talks trash to the ref while FoN gets to his feet and circles behind Razor. Razor finally finishes jawing at the ref, and turns to find FoN, and is quickly met by a running lariat. Razor quickly gets to his feet, but is met by an armdrag take down, which FoN turns into an armlock submission maneuver. FoN keeps the hold on until Razor manages to squirm to the ropes the wrestlers break and both men circle in the ring. They lock up and the bigger Razor throws FoN off. Razor jaws to the crowd and flexes and is drawing a sizable amount of boos. FoN gets up and hooks the leg of Razor from behind and attempts to end the match with a small package 12 Razor kicks out. Both men spring to their feetFoN kicks Razor in shins, then hits him with a swinging neck breakerOut of the back, a 50 year old man wearing a gray suit makes his way towards the ring Dave: I don't like or understand this. That is Prisoner 61185's manager, Warden Kenneth Widger, what is he doing out here? There is a shot of Jason Chase sitting in the front row of the stands, yawning. FoN stomps Razor once and then begins to climb the ropes. Warden Widger quickens his pace to the ring FoN heads to the top and looks like he is going to go for a diving head butt he dives, but Razor rolls out of the way just in timeboth men are down on the canvas. Finally Razor gets up and pulls FoN up by his hair the referee admonishes Razor for pulling FoN's hair. Razor whips FoN towards the ropes, and then jaws at referee Buzz Meacham. When FoN reaches the ropes, Warden Widger jumps up onto the ring apron and whacks him across the back with a nightstick. FoN staggers away from the ropes, only to be demolished by a spear from Razor. Razor gives the sign for The Razor's Edge. He picks up FoN in a crucifix, then hits the Razor's Edge. Warden Widger is loving every minute of it as referee Buzz Meacham counts 1....2.....3! Winner: Razor Dave: Razor, picking up a tainted win over the Freak of Nature here in the debut match of PCW. Steven: I have no problem with the way he won that match. Sometimes in the world of wrestling it is not what you know, but who you know that can make the difference between the feeling the thrill of victory and the agony of defeat. Studio retrospective: Dave and Steven reflect on PCW's first year, then Jeff Marx joins (We cut away from the arena and go to a studio in Atlantic City. In the studio, at a table, are Dave Kern and Steven Smith.) Dave: And on September 22nd of the year 2000, with the long-gone Razor’s victory over the late Freak of Nature, PCW was rolling. Hello everybody, I am Dave Kern. I am being joined by my broadcast partner for that inaugural show, Steven Smith. Steven: Hello Dave: On tonight’s special edition of Sabotage, we look back at the year of PCW. Last night was PCW’s one year anniversary. Tonight, we celebrate it by showing you some classic matches, great feuds, and other historic PCW moments over the past 12 months. Steven: It doesn’t feel the same without Jeffy. Dave: Well, Jeff wasn’t part of the team yet. For the first month, it was just Steven Smith and I doing the announcing. Then, on October 22nd, the night of PCW Dawning, MWF announcers Mark Malloy and Jeff Marx invaded, as did numeroud MWF wrestlers, and took our jobs for the night. (A brief clip of Malloy and Marx taking over for Kern and Smith at Dawning on 10/22/00 is shown) Dave: Immediately afterwards, for the October 27th edition of Fusion, a tag team match was made between myself and Steven against Mark and Jeff. Steven: I remember that match. That hurt like poop. Dave: Hurt like poop? Steven: Jeff hit me with some stiff shots. But I enjoy Jeff being stiff around me. Dave sighs Dave: Let’s take you back to October 27th. Myself and Steven take on Jeff and Mark to determine who the PCW announce team would be. (We cut to the tape of Friday Fusion on October 27th) Mark Malloy and Jeff Marx come out first and step into the ring. Marx continually whispers something into Malloy's ear and Malloy nods his head in agreement. Clay Clayborne: This match is to determine who will be the PCW announce team. In the ring at this time, hailing from Baltimore, Maryland, at a combined weight of 375 lbs, Mark Malloy and Jeff Marx. The crowd boos Marx heartily. The lights grow dim and "Bad to the Bone" from George Thoroughgood and the Delaware Destroyers blasts across the PA as Steven Smith and Dave Kern make their way to the ring. Clay Clayborne: And their opponents, weighing in a combined weight of 400 lbs, Dave Kern and Steven Smith!! Steven and Dave enter the ring. Marx starts talking trash to Smith immediately. There is a camera in the back showing about half the PCW roster grabbing weapons to stage interferences. (literally) Then, it happens. A completely enclosed steel cage is lowered around the ring sealing all competitors inside and voiding the chance of interferences. Marx and Smith start it off. Smith slaps Marx and Marx slaps him back. Smith cries. Marx kicks smith in the shin. Smith cries more, this time hopping around on one foot, and by proving he can cry and hop around on one foot at the same time, he proves he handle multiple tasks, which will look good on his resume if he loses this match and has to search for work. Marx hits Smith with a clothes line and drops him. Smith is sobbing. Marx bounces off the ropes and executes a leg drop. He goes for the cover1.2.kickout. Marx is winded. He taps out to Mark Malloy. Mark Malloy comes into the ring. He picks up Smith and whips him into the ropes. He bends down and goes for a back body drop, but gets a kick to the mouth instead. Smith taps out to Kern. Kern puts Malloy into a headlock. Kern begins to run towards the corner while holding Malloy's head, in an attempt to ram his head into turnbuckle. Malloy manages to push his way free and shoves Kern into the corner with force. Kern stumbles out of the corner and Malloy hits him with a swinging neck breakerare you kidding me? Malloy with the cover.12.NO!!! Malloy's partner Jeff Marx breaks the cover, telling Malloy to let him do it. The ref orders Marx out of the ring. Malloy tags Marx. Marx in turn, slips on a pair of brass knucks and "tags" his partner Mark Malloy in the back of the head and covers his own partner in the corner. The ref looks confused..1..2.3!!! Winners: Dave Kern and Steven Smith.AND Jeff Marx? Marx and Smith embrace in the middle of the ring. Kern looks shocked and disturbed. He checks on Mark Malloy. Bobby: Uh, why would Marx eliminate his own partner and cost himself a job? Jason: Damn it, back to the AOL chatroom with my plebian ass either way. The cage is raised. Steven Smith and Jeff Marx come to the announce table. Marx threatens both Bradford and Maddux with is brass knucks and they leave. Marx and Smith take up their positions at the broadcast table.Jeff: Ah yes, payback is sweet. Oh, at home, you are wondering what happened? Well, let's just say that the PCW has a three man announce team now, Dave, Steven and myself. Malloy was a hack, and he is gone now, so do not worry about him anymore. Steven: (still sniffling) Those were some pretty stiff shots you were giving me out there. Dave still looks stunned as he exits the ring. Paramedics wheel away Mark Malloy. (We cut back to our Atlantic City studio) Dave: And that is how our announce team came about. Jeff Marx did the unthinkable and turned on his longtime partner to join the PCW team. With that, allow me to introduce the third member of our announce team, Jeff Marx. (“Quiet” by Smashing Pumpkins plays in the background as Jeff Marx comes into the studio. Steven gets up from his seat and goes for a hug, but Jeff smacks him instead. Marx takes a seat.) Jeff: Don’t ever try hugging me again! Steven: But you hugged me then. Jeff: I didn’t hug you because I like you, or because I believe in your faggot ways. I only did it because I was happy for the SWERVE that I just pulled off. Dave: So now you’re taking all the credit for that. Jeff: Yep. I was the mastermind behind the whole thing. Dave sighs Jeff: But I will give you some credit. It’d be hard to imagine PCW without you two backing me up on commentary. Dave: Did you just say something good about us? Jeff: If you wanna think of it as that, then be my guest. It’d be hard to imagine a single man announce team. I’m not saying I WANT you guys backing me up, I’m just saying that it’d be hard to imagine PCW without you two. Dave: I knew there was some kind of catch behind it. Steven: You really do love me. I knew it! SMACK! Dave: The three of us have had some great moments together. But the one moment with the three of us that sticks out the most was at Shanghaied. Jeff: You mean when Steven wasn’t allowed in. Dave: That’s the one. We’re gonna cut to commercial. But before we go to break, here is the opening to PCW Shanghaied featuring myself Jeff, and Steven. The same opening that was later ripped off by a rip-off federation named Five Star Wrestling. Shanghaied passport screening: Chinese officials detain Steven Smith at the airport (We cut to the opening from PCW Shanghaied on 1/27/01) A mass of people is surging towards the entrance officials trying to get through the entrance process as quickly and painlessly as possible. Jeff: Jesus, this line is moving slow. Dave: There's a line here? Just looks a pushing mob of people to me. Steven: (huffing) You'd think "these people" would have a good concept of how to make a line, after all those years of waiting in line for bread. Jeff: That's Russia, you jackass. After a few moments, the trio make it to the entrance officer. The entrance officer is sitting behind a simple counter, wearing the green uniform and emblems of the Chinese Army. Beyond the officer, on the other side of the entrance gate, stand two round faced, unattractive women and an older, stern faced man, also wearing army uniforms. Dave Kern is the first one in line. Entrance Officer: Passport. Dave smiles and hands over his passport. EO: What is the purpose of your stay in China, Mr. Kern? Dave: Business. EO: What kind of business? Dave: Entertainment. The EO types some words into a computer. After a few moments hands Dave back his passport without even looking at him and waves him through. Dave passes by the watchful eye of the two, round faced women and stern looking older man, then stops and waits for his colleagues. Jeff Marx is the next to approach the EO. EO: Passport. Jeff hands over his passport. EO: You also here for entertainment business, Mr. Marx? Jeff nods. The EO types into his computer and after a few moments hands Jeff back his passport and waves him through. The camera focuses back on Steven Smith, who is noticeably sweating. The EO beckons him to approach. Steven obliges. EO: Passport. Steven hands over his passport and begins to look up at the ceiling of the airport nervously. EO: Purpose of your visit, Mr. Smith? Steven: Business. Like my two friends over there. Jeff: Uh, we are not your friends. Dave elbows Jeff. The EO begins typing at his computer terminal. The camera focuses in on Steven's sweaty face, as he uses a hankerchief to dab himself off. The shot then shifts back to the EO who seems to be waiting for information on his screen. After a moment, the EO's eyes narrow, and he types some more keys. The camera shifts back to the eyes of Steven Smith, which are getting glassy. Then, the camera shifts over to Dave and Jeff, who are looking at each other in confusion. Finally the EO looks back at the sternfaced man. EO: Wang shen shen! Guo lai! The older man approaches the EO's computer terminal. The EO points to the screen, which is facing away from the camera so we can not see it contents. The two Chinese officials whisper back and forth rapidly to each other, and their eyes keep darting to and from Steven. Jeff is shown in the background craning his neck to see what is on the computer screen, to no avail. Finally the olderman speaks. Loyal Communist Party Member Wang (pronounced Wong): (thick Chinese accent, stilted tones) I am Officer Wang. Mista Smith, I am so solly, but there is a pwoblem with your entrance visa. You may not enter. Steven: WHAT?! Why not? Wang: Your visa was issued by our consulate in the US in ewwor. You may not pass. Steven: Why not?! Wang: You are a known perpetwator of activities considered illegal in China. It is our powericy to not arrow your kind entrance. Steven: My kind? What is THAT supposed to mean? Jeff (whispering to Dave): Maybe China isn't so bad after all. Dave: (angrily, whispering) Would you shut up all ready! Wang: Prease Mista Smith, do not make this difficult. You may not pass. Steven: (miffed, crossing his arms) Hmmph! I will pass! I want to see your manager! Wang: I am the manager. Steven: I want to see the director of airport security! Wang: You are looking at him. Steven: (panicking) I am an American citizen! You can not refuse me entrance! I demand to see my congressman!! I will not be oppressed!! Wang looks back over his shoulder at the two round faced women and nods. The two women, who may or may not be twins, but either way, we know the parents that spawned each of them were not blessed with good looks, nod back then spring into action. Dave: Uh oh! The two round faced move towards Steven with blinding speed. One goes high, while the other goes low. Steven tosses his carry on bag at one of the women to slow her progress, but she bats it aside with her forearm, sending the bag to the airport floor causing its contents to be spilled onto the floor, revealing a tube of KW jelly, a strand of beads and a picture of Boca del Inferno, with the lower half of it digitally blurred. The two women wrestle Steven to the ground and easily subdue the portly, openly homosexual announcer. Steven: I demand to see my congressman!! I am an American!! The two round faced women then drag Steven off into a side room and the door slams shut behind them with a thundering metallic boom. Wang turns and looks to the floor, at the spilled contents of Steven's bag. His eyes show signs of disgust, as he waves over an airport janitor, and motions her to take the stuff away. Wang then turns to the horrified duo of Dave Kern and Jeff Marx. Slowly but surely, an facial feature probably completely unaccustomed from Wang wrenches its way to the old man's face. A smile. Wang: (insincerely, to Dave and Jeff) Welcome to the People's Republic of China. Enjoy your stay. The shot focuses in on Wang's wicked looking smile as the scene fades to black. (Commercial: PCW is brought to you by Red October. PCW Red October: Live and only on PPV. Call your local cable or satellite provider to order NOW!) Dave: Welcome back to the PCW Anniversary Special. A trademark of PCW over the last year has been their unique giant brawls. Jeff: Also known to us as clusterfucks. Dave: FAMILY SHOW! Jeff: So what? This is PCW’s one-year anniversary. Let it all hang out. Steven: I’ll let it all hang out for you after the show, Jeff. SMACK! Dave: It all started with the Carnival of Carnage back in October. When PCW shut down for about a week in late-June, PCW officials decided to make a mark in the “new” PCW the same way they did in the “old” PCW. How did they do this? They held another Carnival of Carnage match appropriately named Carnival of Carnage 2. Steven: This wasn’t as good as the original. It didn’t have Ulty. Dave: Which one was better is debatable. Anyways, for your viewing pleasure, from the July 7th edition of Sunday Sabotage, the Carnival of Carnage 2 match. Jeff: And if you think this is the only clusterfuck we’re showing, then you’re dead wrong. We’ve got about 50 more of these things to go. Dave sighs Dave: let’s go to the match. Carnival of Carnage 2 (We cut to a tape of the 7/7/01 edition of Sunday Sabotage) The scene shifts to Tully's Amusement Arena on the Boardwalk in Virginia Beach. Unlike the last Carnival of Carnage which took place in October, the Marina is open for business and is full of paying customers. Dave: This looks like it could get dangerous. Last time the park was closed. Jeff: What's a few collateral damages when the off chance of some kid getting accidentally throttled is worth an extra .5 in the ratings just on people's freakish need to hope for another human being to be injured. Dave: What the hell are you talking about? Jeff: I suppose you are one of these people that think auto racing is so popular because people like to see cars race around a circular track? Dave: That's exactly why it is popular! Jeff: (making a loud buzzing noise) BBUUUUUUZZZZZZ!!! Incorrect! The reason people watch that boring ass NASCAR is on the freakish hope someone dies...Like Dale Earnhardt for instance. Ratings for NASCAR spiked when he bit the big one. There is stunned silence for 1......2......3......4......5 seconds. Dave: (coldly) You have no soul. Steven: Speaking of no soul! The camera suddenly shifts to a merry go round, where a small child of about five years old is enjoying her time, going up and down on the bobbing horse. Steven: There is something so sexual about the imagery there. Suddenly the child's joyful expression turns to one of horror as a blood curdling scream of "Die! Die! Die! Die! Die! Die! Die!" rings forth. A blur of movement enters the picture, plowing the girl off the horse. The scene shifts to the faces of the horrified faces of the parents of the young girl, then whips back around and pans out, to reveal the girl on the ground flat on his back, with Bethany on top of her. Dave: OH MY GOD!!!! Bethany grabs the young girl by the hair and begins smashing her head into the wooden platform of the merry go round. Bethany: Die! Die! Die! Die! Die! Die! Die! The girl's parents try to rush up onto the platform to save their daughter from the horrific beating she is taking at the hands of Bethany, but they are cut off by Clyde, Darkstar and Stryfe, so they can only watch as Bethany continues to smash away. Dave: This is hooooooorible!!! Jeff: This is great!!!! Steven: Look at them horses go up...and down...up...and down...up and down. We now find ourselves looking at a very large, black cannon. A sign nearby reads "Everyone come see THE HUMAN BULLET! Performing in one hour!". Casually, Kid Wonder and Amanda stroll by this sign hand in hand, with KW looking sarcastically at the cannon. Wonder: Feh, I dont need a cannon to fly. "Hey kids! Over here!" Kid Wonder and Amanda spin to face PCW's evil super genius Alan Kriegman and his raven-haired dominatrix wife, Barbara Bush-Kriegman. Kriegman grins rather evilly, his arm around the waist of his wife. Kriegman: So, you two think you're PCW's First Couple, huh? I'm sorry, but that position is TAKEN! Wonder: I dont think so! C'mon Amanda, let's show these losers who the first couple really is! With that, KW and Amanda run at Alan and Barbara, who in turn begin to flee towards the giant cannon. KW and Amanda give chase to the Kriegmans, who are running up some stairs to the barrell of the cannon. Atop the stairs, Barbara quickly jumps into the barrell of the cannon, with Kid Wonder following in pursuit. Meanwhile, Kriegman has slid down the barrel, and is now standing at the back end, where a single fuse is attaches, unlit. Out of a hatch pops Barbara Bush, who scurries out and slams it shut. A loud banging comes from within, behind the hatch. KW: Hey, lemme outta here! Kriegman looks toward his wife with an evil grin. Kriegman: You thinking what I'm thinking? Barbara: Got matches? Kriegman: Heh, yep. The evil super genius pulls a book of matches out of his suit jacket, lights one, and puts it to the fuse of the giant cannon containing Kid Wonder. KW: What's that hissing sound? The only response Kid Wonder recieves is an ENORMOUS, THUNDEROUS BOOM when the Cannon goes off, sending Kid Wonder high into the sky, Alan Kriegman laughing evilly. Amanda looks on in horror, running off in the direction of her flying boyfriend. The scene shifts to where Argyle is shown walking through the petting zoo section of the park. Argyle spots a lovely teenage girl who is working as a park worker and approaches here. Argyle: Excuse me, can you tell me if there are any sheep in this petting zoo? Girl: Why yes, just past the goats and llamas around the bend there. Argyle: Great! Are they big, white and wooly? Girl: (kinda weirded out) Yeah, I suppose. Argyle: With nice wide hips? Girl: Er, I never really looked at the width of their hips. Argyle: Oh nevermind, I will check for myself. Argyle skips down the lane of the petting zoo until he reaches the sheep pen. Argyle: (drooling) Fluffy sheep.....soooooo sooooftt....... Argyle climbs into the pen and begins petting the hind quarters of one of the larger sheep. Suddenly there is a huge crash and a scream! "I knew I'd find you here!!!" The camera pans out as a Doug Troy clocks Argyle in the head with a set of electric sheep sheers, knocking him to the ground next to his new sheep friend. To add insult to injury, the prudish sheep Argyle was making "friends with" suddenly lashes out, nailing Argy in the skull with a kick. DT quickly drops down and makes the cover on the stunned Argy as the PFIK counts...1...2...3!!! ARGYLE HAS BEEN ELIMINATED!!! Doug Troy gets up, looks at the camera and smiles. DT: That was almost too easy! Suddenly, a blur of motion enters the scene. Dave: What the? The camera pans out to reveal an unidentified man whipping DT with one of those rubber snakes they give out as prizes for carnival games. Jeff: Who the heck is THAT? Steve: Wait a minute... Jeff: Nah, it can't be... Dave: IT IS!!!!! Dave, Jeff and Steven: (in unison) IT'S HANK~! Hank slaps DT with the rubber snake, then slingshots him into the side of the sheep's pen, sending DT up and over the fence. Hank wastes little time in jumping over the railing, landing ass first upon DT's face. Dave: He calls that The Rank Stank of Hank!!!! DT's eyes roll up into the back of his head and he stops moving. PFIK lifts DT's arm......it falls......he lifts it again......and once again it falls....... Finally for third time......No sign of life. PFIK shakes his head no and waves off DT. DOUG TROY HAS BEEN ELIMINATED!!! Jeff: DOUG TROY JUST DID THE J-O-B TO HANK AGAIN!!!! Dave: What was that! Jeff: Hank I say!!! Hank!! Hank climbs to his feet and begins to celebrate his victory...but the celebration is short lived, as a sandal wearing foot suddenly enters the picture, nailing HANK in the jaw. Hank goes down like a ton of bricks. Dave: OH MY GOD!!! The camera pans out to reveal the sandal, and the foot that was inside of it, belong to noneother than Jobber Supremo Extraordinare, Lightning Kid. LK looks down at the unmoving Hank and makes the cover...1...2...3!!!! HANK HAS BEEN ELIMINATED!!!!! Jeff: This is pathetic. LK hops to his feet, only to be suddenly dragged back down to the ground. Dave: What now?!?! The camera angle widens to show someone has rolled up LK!!! PFIK makes the count...1...2...3!!! LIGHTNING KID HAS BEEN ELIMINATED!!!! Dave: Who the hell is that?!?!?! The person responsible for rolling up LK gets to his feet, turns and faces the camera. Dave: Gasp!!!! Jeff: (apathetically) Jesus... Steve: ROOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOODYYYYYYYY PPPPPPPPOOOOOOOOO!!! Indeed, UFW Ironman/Jobber Roody Poo smiles at the camera, then he runs off. Rich Cannon, Jason Folts and Seth Jackson are shown standing in an impossibly long line waiting for some tasty, lard fried funnel cake. Cannon: This blows, eh? Lines in America are so long. Folts: Right, on. Look at all these Hosers. Jackson: Would you two stop talking like a couple of dumbasses and keep your eyes open already? Cannon and Folts look at each other and begin to grumble. Cannon: Hey Jake, you want a lifesaver? Folts: Sure? Is it cherry? Cannon: No, wintergreen. Folts: It's better than nothing. I'll take it. Cannon pulls out a nearly finished roll of Lifesavers, and gives one to Folts. He then pops out the last one for himself, and discards the wrapper on the ground. Jackson looks down and sighs. Jackson: Rich, dude, littering is definitely not cool. A man in front of the group of Canadian turns around and faces them. His long black hair, and weathered, copper skinned face give away the fact he is of native American descent. He stares at the group, as a tear rolls down his face. The sound of Jeff audibly vomiting can be heard in the background. Cannon sighs and picks up the wrapper. He spots a nearby dumpster. Cannon: Hey guys, hold my spot in line while I through this away. Jackson and Folts pay little attention to Cannon as he walks towards the dumpster. Cannon goes over to the dumpster and tosses in the Lifesaver wrapper and begins to walk away....until something catches his eye. Cannon pauses, then looks back inside the dumpster. The camera angle changes to show the dumpster appears to be completely empty, other than the Lifesaver wrapper, and what appears to be some kind of wrestling title belt. Cannon looks around to see if anyone is looking, then reaches in and grabs the belt by the leather strap. As he pulls it out of the trashcan, he sees the belt is labled "PCW Extreme Champion." Excitedly, Cannon straps the belt around his waste. Cannon: Looks good. I wonder why it was left out here? Cannon looks down and spots a smudge of dirt on the metallic plate on the front of the belt. He brings his hands to his mouth, and reaches down to rub the smudge off, but as soon as his hand comes in contact with the metal..... ZAAAAAP!!!! Sparks fly everywhere and Cannon's hand stands on end. Cannon falls to the ground and begins to shake violently. Jeff: What the hell was that?!? Dave: LOOOK!!! Suddenly, from behind the dumpsters walks Alan Kriegman, with the PALM PILOT OF DEATH in hand. Naket begins laughing with joy, the moment of happiness suddenly coming to an end when he realizes that Bethany is still locked into his throat. Finally, Naket's eyes roll up in the back of his head and he passes out, Bethany landing on top of him. 1...2...3! SCOTT NAKET HAS BEEN ELIMINATED...BY BETHANY! Bethany finally removes herself from Scott Naket, revealing a bloody wound on the Canadian Icon's neck. Grinning a red grin, Bethany surveys the carnage left by the Naket/Dark Age incident, when suddenly... "What the hell...oh, Hi Bethany." Bethany hisses again and apins to face a somewhat apprehensive Dan Hollywood. Before he can react, Dan Hollywood suddenly wails when Bethany latches herself into his neck. Hollywood tries his hardest to remove her, but he gives up much sooner than Naket, and collapses...right on top of Bethany. 1...2...3! BETHANY HAS BEEN ELIMINATED! The cameras cut to a large go kart track on the outskirts of the park. Towering over a group of eleven-year-olds are Badge, Agent Orange, and Roody Poo, waiting in line with tickets in hand. The three men glare at eachother, nervously drumming their fingers on the guard rail. Finally, the current race ends, and a pack of kids disembarks. The line surges forward, and Badge, tired of waiting, begins picking kids up by their shirts and depositing them behind him. Agent Orange and Roody Poo shrug and follow in his wake. The three take their seats -- Agent Orange in an orange kart, Badge in a blue kart, and Roody Poo in a silver one. While waiting for the race to start, Badge reaches into his pocket and produces a small, portable police siren, which he Velcros to the roof of the kart. Grinning and tensely gripping the wheel, he leans forward and prepares to gun it. Announcer: On your mark! Get set! GO! The race karts roar into life and rocket forward. Badge allows himself to fall behind the back a bit, then reaches up to the roof and flicks a switch. The police siren begins to wail, and the small dome light begins to flash blue and red. Grinning even more, he pulls forward, not-too-gently slamming into the kids in front of him and knocking their karts around. One of the kids, barely able to stay in control, glares at the corrupt cop. Kid: Hey, watch it, mister! In reply, Badge rams him again, knocking him off the track. Up ahead, Roody Poo races neck-and-neck with a cute little blond girl with pig tails and a pink dress. Roody, smiling benevolently, calls over to her. Roody Poo: Nice racing, little girl! Girl: SHUTCHER F*CKING MOUTH, SH*TF*CKER! YA GONNA RACE OR WHAT?! Roody, suprised, allows himself to fall back a bit. Agent Orange, trailing right behind him, slams into Roody's rear bumper and sends him off to the side. Growling in determination, Orange guns it, coming up even with the pig-tailed little girl. The little blond turns to him, a snarl on her lips. Girl: Don't old f*cks like you have better things to do than this?! What the f*ck are you, some kinda assf*cker?! Agent Orange frowns, pulls next to the girl, and punches her in the face. The girl loses control for a second, but then comes back and smashes into Orange's kart. The two begin ramming eachother repeatedly, while Roody Poo looks for an opening. The camera cuts to the rear of the pack, where Badge, apparently not too concerned about winning, simply smashes into every kid in his way. Most of them, seeing the trail of destruction the cop leaves in his wake, attempt to get to the sides and out of the way. This, of course, just makes it easier for Badge to knock them off the track. Suddenly, two more clowns, shaped much like JJ and Trashcan Man, jump out from behind a booth, right in the path of the Russian bear. The cameras cut to Tommy Christopher, who strolls casually through the amusement park, checking out the sights. As he walks, he notices a large, unoccupied dunk tank (a device in which a person sits in a chair over a vat of water, and people throw balls at a target. If they hit, the unfortunate in the chair gets dunked). Curious, he walks over to examine it, and finds the glass door to the chair unlocked. Tommy: Ooh...padded leather. Looks comfortable. Christopher steps inside, and takes a seat, shifting a bit until he gets comfortable. After a few seconds, he closes his eyes, and begins to drift off to sleep. The glass door suddenly slams shut, and Tommy pulls himself to his feet. Outside, grinning from ear to ear and holding a softball is The Arch. The camera cuts to outside a massive, pinstriped pavillion, with a large sign reading "Hall of Mirrors" hanging above the entrance. Stealthily sneaking into it, dressed in their PWA Instructor T-shirts, are BJ Mac, Jack Valentine, and the White Mexican. The three men push aside the tent flap, and step inside. Upon entering, they look around in amazement at dozens of reflections of themselves. Jack Valentine: DAMN, I look good. BJ Mac: All right, we're gonna split up and take defensive positions in here. Eventually, everyone's gonna come in here, and when they do, we jump them. After everyone's gone, we fight eachother. Got it? Jack Valentine: Yeah. White Mexican: Yo. The three split up, each carefully wandering off to a hiding place and making sure to remember the way they came. Almost immediately after they find hiding spots, the lean, black-suited figure of Alan Kriegman struts into the pavillion. He walks over to the nearest mirror, looks into it, and adjusts his tie. As he fidgets over himself, the White Mexican begins stalking towards him, not noticing his reflection in several mirrors. Alan Kriegman: Hmm...yes, you are an evil stud, aren't you? A lay-dee killah, baby. Ed has NOTHING on you. Nothing! Suddenly, White Mexican pounces, roaring a battle cry and hoping to catch the evil super genius off-guard. Kriegman, noticing his reflection, cooly sidesteps out of the way as WM plunges head-first into the mirror, shattering it. In one smooth motion, Kriegman pulls the Palm Pilot...of DEATH out of his coat pocket, spins around, and fires off two shots. One hits BJ Mac squarely in the chest, dropping him instantly. The other narrowly misses Jack Valentine, reflecting off a mirror and ricocheting throughout the Hall. Kriegman: I HATE it when I miss. The evil supergenius fires again, and Jack, expecting it this time, rolls under the shot and comes up in front of Kriegman, who drops the Palm Pilot...of DEATH to the floor and raises his fists. Valentine's face pales visibly as the evil supergenius reviews the list of options. Kriegman: "Frappe"..."Charbroil"... Ah, here we are. Valentine gulps. Kriegman: "Spank." Grinning as evilly as ever, Kriegman presses the button. A pinkish-red beam fires out of the Palm Pilot...of DEATH, striking the prone Valentine directly on the bum. Jack howls again, leaping to his feet and clutching his backside with both hands and begging for mercy. The PP..oD flies out of his hands, clattering across the floor and into the depths of the Hall of Mirrors. Jack Valentine, breathing a sigh of relief, collapses. The unknown intruder looks at Kremmen, and TTD, who is wobbly getting up. The scar on the side of the invader's face is glistening in the arena lights. He grins at them, his right eye squinting, and charges at Kremmen. He tackles the man down to the ground and then rolls back to a stance as TTD comes to him. They stand nearly eye to eye, with only an inch or so in difference. TTD swings his fist, as the man ducks under it, drilling him in the stomach. TTD hits the mat as the man picks Kremmen up. The man roars at TTD and flexes his arms forward, his huge body rippling into something that looks chiseled out of granite. With TTD's back turned, it leaves him open for another Reverse DVD. The man lifts the 300 pounder up onto his shoulders and drills his head into the mat. He rolls out of the ring and walks up the ramp slowly. The cameraman zooms in on his shoulders where the word 'MEANSTREAK' is tattooed across them. He reaches the entryway and turns, looking at the path of destruction he just caused, grins madly, and heads up the ramp towards the entryway. During the mayhem, Scott Naket appears and covers The Chef..1...2...3!!!! THE CHEF HAS BEEN ELIMINATED!!! Bertha is still spinning Seraph around on her shoulders. Naket comes over and kicks her in her ample gut, causing her to fall backwards and drop Seraph. Seraph hits the ground hard, and Naket clobbers him with the iron skillet..He makes the cover..1...2...3!!! SERAPH HAS BEEN ELIMINATED!!! Kremmen begins to climb down the ladder. When he gets to the bottom, he looks around for Alan Kriegman, but doesn't see him. Back at the Hell Hole, Boda and Meanstreak are both looking pale. Boda vomits, but it just floats in the middle of the ride. Big J tells David to stop the ride, but there is no answer. He looks over and sees that David is no longer in the control booth. Big J tells Goliath to go over an investigate. As Goliath gets there, he sees David has been laid out, with broken glass near his head. He goes into check what happened, and all of a sudden, Alan Kriegman comes swings in from the other end of the booth and kick Goliath in the chest. Goliath stumbles back, and teeters on the edge of the platform, then falls into the ride, along with Boda and Meanstreak. Kriegman gives the camera an evil grin as he turns the ride's speed dial up to maximum speed. He laughs maniacally then covers David.1....2....3!!! DAVID HAS BEEN ELIMINATED!!!! Back in the house of mirrors, O.d is brandishing a monkey wrench, and Doug Troy has holding a trash can lid in his left hand as a shield and a Singapore Cane in his right hand. O.d charges forward and swings, but DT blocks the blow with his trash can lid and gives O.d a forearm to the chops. O.d stumbles back into a mirror and it breaks. DT follows drops the lid and gives O.d a two handed cane shot to the skull. O.d smiles. DT looks confused. DT hits him again with the cane to the head. O.d smiles and asks form more. Finally DT hits him twice in succession, and O.d just laughs. Finally, DT tosses the cane aside and kicks O.d in the nadular region and O.d doubles over. DT locks sets O.d up for The Take, then executes it! He goes for the cover...1....2...3!!! O.d HAS BEEN ELIMINATED!!! (With a smile on his face) Doug Troy stumbles out of the house of mirrors. He finally finds the exit and steps outside, just to be clubbed over the head three times in succession by a kendo stick. DT goes down. Kremmen makes the cover1...2...3!!! DOUG TROY HAS BEEN ELIMINATED!!! Big J is confused about what he should do. He sees he is now alone, and Kriegman has control of the ride. Finally Big J points to Kriegman "You want into the Serial Thrillaz?" says Big J. Kriegman nods approvingly. Kriegman stops the ride. Meanstreak, Boda and Goliath all stumble off the walls and fall to the floor in the middle. Big J jumps down and lines the three of them up and covers all three....1....2....3!!! BODA, MEANSTREAK AND GOLIATH HAVE BEEN ELIMINATED!!!! Suddenly the ride begins to spin. Big J and the referee look at each other with looks of dread, as Kriegman looks down on them with an evil grin. He waves to the pair, then shifts the ride on! Kremmen arrives on the at the Hell Hole at the same time as Scott Naket. Kremmen has his kendo stick while Naket is brandishing the iron skillet. Kriegman steps down from the ride platform. Big J screams for help from the ride. Finally, he vomits. Then he screams for help one more time, then says "I give up!! I give up!! Just turn the ride off!" A referee comes over and grants Big J his wish. BIG J HAS BEEN ELIMINATED!!! Kremmen, Scott Naket and Alan Kriegman circle. Kremmen makes the first move and comes at the "weaponless" Kriegman. Kriegman suddenly produces some mace and sprays it in Kremmen's eyes. Kremmen stumbles back, and Kriegman charges in, but Kremmen has the presence of mind to put up a big boot, catching Kriegman in the chopsKriegman stumbles back and takes an iron skillet shot to the face from Naket. Naket makes the cover...1....2...3.!!! ALAN KRIEGMAN HAS BEEN ELIMINATED!!! Kremmen is still blinded. Naket spits on his hands and rubs them together, then picks up the iron skillet. Though blind, Kremmen still looks dangerous with the Kendo stick. All of a sudden there is a flash of headlights. A yellow hummer comes out of nowhere rams into Kremmen. He Kremmen bounces off the hood and rolls over the top of the car and tumbles to the hard wood. The hummer screeches to a halt and out jumps "The Future" Ryan Knox with a baseball bat. He screams to Naket RK: "Pin him, then give me a title shot on Tuesday at Turmoil!" Naket seems cautious, holding his iron skillet up high. RK: Pin him, we can play tired Scott Naket brandishing a iron skillet, vs fresh Ryan Knox wielding a baseball bat! Then I will sit here to see if he'll take my offer when he is conscious. Naket shrugs, and covers Kremmen.1...2...3!!! Winner and New PCW Extreme Champion: Scott Naket (we cut back to the announce team in Atlantic City) Jeff: Now I see why the Extreme title has no respect. Dave sighs Dave: That does it for us tonight. The last year has been a magical joy ride with its ups and downs, but still special none the less. Hopefully the 2nd year of PCW will bring just as much excitement as the first. For Steven Smith and Jeff Marx, I’m Dave Kern. Good night and happy anniversary. Dave and Jeff look at the camera while Steven waives rather faggot like to the camera as we fade to black. ©2001 Primetime Championship Wrestling
